## situation_cyberization_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_cyberization_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很快就能准备好用机械部件来强化我们的生物躯体了。大脑的处理能力和肢体的强度可以得到提升。问题是谁会从这种强化改造中得到最多好处呢。\n\n§Y次月获得植入体的人口数§!：§G[From.GetCyberPopsPerMonth]§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:5`

### English

We will soon be ready to augment our biological bodies with machine parts. The processing power of the brain and the strength of limbs can be increased. The question is who will benefit the most from such augmentation.\n\n§YPops to receive implants next month§!: §G[From.GetCyberPopsPerMonth]§!

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:6`

## building_augmentation_center_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_augmentation_center_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座进行义体植入体安装的建筑。有购物商城，有运营中心，还有机械商店。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:50`

### English

A building for outfitting cybernetic implants. Part shopping mall, part operating center, and part mechanic shop.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:55`

## building_augmentation_center_government_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_augmentation_center_government_desc&lang=en)

官方医务部允许公民选择并安装经批准的义体植入体。

### English

## building_augmentation_center_corporate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_augmentation_center_corporate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个满足你所有义体需求的庞大综合体，配有个人数字购物商场和豪华的术后水疗中心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

A sprawling complex for all your cybernetic needs, complete with personal digital shoppers and a luxurious, post-surgery spa.

## job_cyberdome_spectator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_cyberdome_spectator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$job_cyberdome_spectator_plural$享受着$building_cyberdome$中的比赛。通过义体大脑观赛时，即便是在最便宜的坐席也能看到如此盛景，但如果允许神经广播的话则还可以以第一人称感受角斗士的体验。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

$job_cyberdome_spectator_plural$ enjoy games in the $building_cyberdome$. When witnessed via cyberbrain, even the cheapest seats provide a spectacle, but when neurocasting is enabled it's possible to experience gladiatorial combat in first person.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:112`

## cyber.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

控制未来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

Controlling the Future

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:253`

## cyber.100.desc.shared

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.100.desc.shared&lang=en)

### 中文

最近在高等义体植入体方面的突破有朝一日将产生重塑社会的可能性。我们可以消除一切生物学弱点，变得更快、更强、更聪明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

Recent breakthroughs in advanced cybernetic implants will one day make it possible to reshape society. By eliminating any biological weakness, we can make ourselves smarter, faster, stronger.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:254`

## cyber.100.desc.shared_unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.100.desc.shared_unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

最近在高等义体植入体方面的突破有朝一日将产生重塑社会的可能性，这次将由我们自己掌控。通过消除现有增强技术中的生物学缺陷和弱点，我们可以使自己变得更聪明、更快、更强壮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

Recent breakthroughs in advanced cybernetic implants will one day make it possible to reshape society, this time on our own terms. By eliminating biological weaknesses and defects in our existing augments, we can make ourselves smarter, faster, stronger.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:255`

## cyber.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.100.desc.shared$\n\n各大企业提出了一些商业提案和销售前景预测，然而政府官员则坚持必须保证由国家来控制这种潜在的技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

$cyber.100.desc.shared$\n\nCorporations have provided business proposals and sales projections, while government officials argue that access to this potential technology must be guaranteed by the state.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:256`

## cyber.100.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.100.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.100.desc.shared_unplugged$\n\n各大企业提出了一些商业提案和销售前景预测，然而政府官员则坚持必须保证由国家来控制这种潜在的技术。

### English

$cyber.100.desc.shared_unplugged$\n\nCorporations have provided business proposals and sales projections, while government officials argue that access to this potential technology must be guaranteed by the state.

## cyber.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

取之于民，用之于民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

By the People, For the People

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:267`

## cyber.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于我们计划的许多义体设计对普通人来说都将会遥不可及，一些自改造者们感到非常愤怒。\n\n他们认为如果公开原理图并放松有关的环境法规，几乎每个人都能制造和修理自己的强化体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

Self-modders are furious that many of our planned cybernetic designs will be inaccessible to the average person.\n\nThey argue that if the schematics were made public and environmental regulations relaxed, almost everyone will be able to manufacture and repair their own augmentations.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:268`

## cyber.105.desc.megacorp

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.105.desc.megacorp&lang=en)

### 中文

独立承包商们抱怨称难以获取我们计划的许多义体设计。\n\n他们认为如果公开蓝图，并将与生产有关的专利放松，将能改善义体的生产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

Independent contractors complain that many of our planned cybernetic designs are inaccessible to them.\n\nThey argue that if the schematics were made available and patents related to production relaxed, implant production could be improved.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:269`

## cyber.120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个研究团队提出了一项新议案，将监管人工智能集成到我们的移动设备中，随后又可以直接整合进义体件中。\n\n这些监管人工智能将会引导我们公民将关注点从无聊的任务转移到更需要脑力的任务上。虽然会因此丧失一部分自主权，但对生产力前所未有的提高将不容忽视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

A team of researchers has put forth an innovative proposal to integrate AI supervisors into our mobile devices. Later these could be incorporated directly into cyberware.\n\nThe supervisors would redirect the focus of our citizenry away from idle tasks towards more cerebral endeavors. While some level of autonomy will be lost, the unprecedented potential to amplify our productivity cannot be overlooked.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:281`

### English

### English

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:290`

## cyber.160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一部广受好评的新剧抓住了公众们的集体想象力。\n\n故事发生在一个义体植入体盛行的世界里，讲述一位强化改造的工人遭遇了与改造身体的创伤性分离。故事的悲剧性结局对完全义体化及其对[Owner.Species.GetAdj]身份不可避免的抹除提出了一种几乎不加掩饰的批评。\n\n这部剧的大获成功引发了对义体化倡议的强烈反对，自该剧播出以来，可用测试对象的注册人数骤减了60%。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

A widely acclaimed new drama has captured the collective imagination of the public.\n\nSet in a world where cybernetic implants are prevalent, the narrative follows an augmented worker experiencing traumatic dissociation from their modified body. The story's tragic conclusion presents a thinly veiled critique of full cyberization and its inevitable erasure of the [Owner.Species.GetAdj] identity.\n\nThe smash hit has sparked significant opposition to our initiative, causing a 60% decrease in available test subjects since the drama's release.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:291`

### 中文

强化改造中心

### English

Augmentation Centers

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:299`

## cyber.200.desc.gov

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.200.desc.gov&lang=en)

### 中文

经过一段时间的政治内斗，生产义体强化件的责任终于被瓜分到到了各个政府部门中。当他们都能分一杯羹时，肯定会互相进行监督。\n\n但计划终究要付诸行动。科研共同体至少需要一座$building_augmentation_center$来作为义体发展的中心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

After a period of political infighting, the responsibilities for producing cybernetic augmentations have been divided between various governmental departments. When everyone gets a piece of the pie, they will surely keep each other in check.\n\nBut plans must be put into action. The research community requires at least one $building_augmentation_center$ to act as a hub for cybernetic development.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:300`

## cyber.200.desc.cor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.200.desc.cor&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经的梦想现在触手可及，无疑我们境内众多的公司能从中大幅获利。义体行业蓬勃发展的一个好处是，新的消费产品推陈出新的速度越来越快了。\n\n但计划终究要付诸行动。企业说客请愿建立一座$building_upload_center$来作为义体发展的中心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

What was once a dream is now within reach, and who better to profit from it than the multitude of corporations within our borders? A benefit of the burgeoning cybernetic industry vigor is that new consumer products can be launched at an accelerated pace.\n\nBut plans must be put into action. The corporate lobbyists request at least one $building_upload_center$ to act as a hub for cybernetic development.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:301`

## cyber.200.desc.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.200.desc.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的通讯网络充斥着各种疯狂的设计构想，从神经突触强化件到义体味觉腺无所不有。其中大多数都非常不切实际，而且实现起来非常昂贵，尽管有些设计看起来还是很有希望的。\n\n但计划终究要付诸行动。线上社群至少需要一座$building_augmentation_center$来作为义体发展的中心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Our communication networks are abuzz with wild design ideas, everything from neuro-synaptic augmentation to cybernetic taste glands. Most of these would be highly impractical and very expensive to implement, though luckily some designs appear promising.\n\nBut plans must be put into action. The online community requires at least one $building_augmentation_center$ to act as a hub for cybernetic development.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:302`

### English

## cyber.210.desc.gov.rival

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.210.desc.gov.rival&lang=en)

### 中文

记者们揭露了政府正在资助涉及思想监控芯片实验的证据。一旦这些设备开发完成，就可以安装在公民的大脑中，收集详细的安全和人格数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Journalists have uncovered proof of government-funded experiments that involve thought-monitoring microchips. Fully developed, these devices could be installed in the minds of citizens to gather detailed security and personality insights.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:323`

## cyber.210.desc.cor.rival

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.210.desc.cor.rival&lang=en)

### 中文

一项与商标纠纷有关的法庭证据表明，企业正在试验增强现实植入体，旨在无缝连接到消费者的视神经上。原理图列出了对用户的好处，还有企业劫持视觉信息的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Court evidence related to a trademark dispute has shown that corporations are experimenting with augmented reality implants, designed to attach seamlessly to a consumer's optical nerve. The schematics outline both the benefits to the user, as well as the ability for a company to hijack visual information.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:324`

## cyber.210.desc.ind.rival

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.210.desc.ind.rival&lang=en)

### 中文

独立的自我改装者已经开始用基本的机械替代品替换肢体。不但没有任何办法可以确保这些自行手术的人的福利，而且人们还担心这种植入体不受管控的应用可能弊大于利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

Independent self-modders have already started replacing limbs with rudimentary mechanical alternatives. There is no way to ensure the welfare of anyone attempting this on their own, and there are fears that unregulated use of implants could do more harm than good.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:325`

## cyber.220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

多肢体协作

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

Multi-Limb Coordination

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:332`

## cyber.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在神经接口领域取得了显著的突破。大脑信号增幅器可以让用户丝滑但有限地操控一组人工肢体。在未来，这种技术也可以直接整合进义体脑中，而不需要外接设备。\n\n在初步测试阶段，测试对象展现出了更高的敏捷性和精确的物体操纵能力，甚至还有更高的战斗熟练程度。然而，对制造成本的担忧促使人们应定夺这项开创性技术应优先运用于什么人群上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

A significant breakthrough has been achieved in the field of neural interfaces. A brain signal amplifier could grant users seamless, but limited control over an array of artificial limbs. Later on, this could be integrated directly to a cyberbrain without the need for an external device.\n\nDuring the initial testing phase, subjects exhibited promising results with dexterity and object manipulation alongside heightened combat proficiency. However, concerns regarding the manufacturing costs have prompted the need to decide who should have first access to this pioneering technology.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:333`

## cyber.260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一款新型脊柱模块的试验以灾难性的失败告终。植入体电机产生的过大力量导致多名测试对象的脊髓遭受永久性损伤。\n\n这一可怕结果的图片被泄露引起了公众的不适，导致未来实验的志愿者严重短缺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

A trial run of a new spinal module has ended in a catastrophic failure. The excess power generated by the implant's motors has resulted in permanent spinal cord injuries for multiple test subjects.\n\nDisturbing images showcasing the horrifying outcome have leaked to the public, causing an acute shortage of volunteers for future experiments.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:343`

### English

Rollout

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:350`

## cyber.300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在全国各地的所有主要城市中心为[Owner.Species.GetName]民众们囤积了大量的机械植入体。这些由$job_augmentor_plural$们设计的装置保证可以大幅提高我们的生理机能。\n\n抛弃过往迎接义体未来的这一天终于到了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

All across our territory, in every major urban center, we have stockpiled mechanical implants for our [Owner.Species.GetName] population. Designed by our $job_augmentor_plural$, these devices promise to greatly enhance our biological forms.\n\nThe day is finally here to cast off our past and embrace a cybernetic future. 

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:351`

## cyber.300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

义体化吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Cyberize.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:352`

## cyber.300.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.300.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

领袖获得特质：§Y$leader_trait_cyborg$§!\n人口获得特质：['trait:trait_cybernetic']\n\n强化改造速度取决于局势应对方案和£building£['building:building_augmentation_center']建筑的数量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Leaders Gain Trait: §Y$leader_trait_cyborg$§!\nPops Gain Trait: ['trait:trait_cybernetic']\n\nAugmentation speed depends on the situation approach and the number of £building£ ['building:building_augmentation_center'] buildings.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:353`

## cyber.305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

义体化反对者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Cybernetic Objectors

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:355`

## cyber.305.desc.shared

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.305.desc.shared&lang=en)

### 中文

在实践中，他们甚至缺乏最基本的植入体，这使得他们难以在我们这个日益紧密联系起来的社会中发挥作用。\n\n强制要求每个人都安装完整的植入体将有助于他们更好地融入社会，但无疑会导致怨恨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

In practice, they lack even the most basic implants, making it difficult to function in our increasingly connected society.\n\nForcing the population to install full implants would help them to better integrate with society, but would undoubtedly lead to resentment.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:356`

## cyber.305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

反对将[Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed]和机械融为一体的宗教团体主动退出了我们义体化人口的行动。$cyber.305.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

Religious groups opposed to the merging of [Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed] and machine have opted out of our efforts to cyberize the population. $cyber.305.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:357`

## cyber.305.desc.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.305.desc.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

不情愿将[Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed]与机械苟同的虔信者们主动退出了我们义体化人口的行动。$cyber.305.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

The faithful, understandably reluctant to merge [Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed] and machine, have opted out of our efforts to cyberize the population. $cyber.305.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:358`

## cyber.305.desc.none

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.305.desc.none&lang=en)

### 中文

将[Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed]和机械融合的道德性是个长久以来为人津津乐道的话题。不幸的是，考虑到义体化带来的效率提高，那些选择退出的人可能处于不利地位。\n\n强迫人们安装植入体可以确保所有人都不会掉队，但这也无疑会导致怨恨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

The morality of merging [Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed] and machine has long been a subject of debate. Unfortunately, given the increased efficiency that results from cyberization, those who opt out may be at a disadvantage.\n\nForcing the population to install implants would ensure that no one is left behind, but it will also undoubtedly lead to resentment.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:359`

## cyber.305.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.305.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

再次融合[Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed]与机器的道德性是个长久以来为人津津乐道的话题。然而，我们自主设计的增强器不再受同化我们的机器摆布，因此效率极高。那些选择推出的人可能会处于不利地位。\n\n强迫人们安装植入体可以确保所有人都不会掉队，但这也无疑会导致怨恨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

The morality of once again merging [Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed] and machine has long been a subject of debate. However, augments of our own design are no longer bound to the whims of the Machine that assimilated us, and as such are exceedingly efficient. Those who opt out may be at a disadvantage.\n\nForcing the population to install implants would ensure that no one is left behind, but it will also undoubtedly lead to resentment.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:360`

## cyber.320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幼体潜力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Infantile Potential

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:370`

## cyber.320.birth.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.birth.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着为新生儿安装植入体的全新技术问世，我们突然陷入了僵局。这些开创性的新生儿强化改造拥有永远改变我们与技术的关系的力量，从幼年开始便解放先天的义体潜力，并将我们的文明推向前所未有的高度。$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

With our newfound capability to insert implants onto newborns, we have suddenly reached an impasse. These pioneering neonatal augmentations possess the power to forever transform our bond with technology, unlocking innate cybernetic potential from an early age and propelling our civilization to unprecedented heights.$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:371`

## cyber.320.metamorphosis.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.metamorphosis.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，我们取得了一项令人难以置信的突破，使微芯片直接植入幼体体内变为可能。在变态发育过程中，我们的幼体获得了一种吸收机械系统的自然能力。在化蛹阶段进行的增强可以释放他们未开发的义体潜力，并将我们的文明推向惊人的新高度。$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Recently, we have made an incredible breakthrough which allows us to implant microchips directly onto our young. During metamorphosis, our larvae have a natural ability to absorb mechanical systems. Augmentations made during the pupal stage can unleash their untapped cybernetic potential and propel our civilization to astonishing new heights.$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:372`

## cyber.320.lithoid.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.lithoid.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着将植入体安装进新结晶后代的全新技术问世，我们突然陷入了僵局。这样的强化改造将使我们的新一代与金属矿物形成一种自然的联系，从很小的时候就释放出他们的义体潜力，并将我们的文明推向惊人的新高度。$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Amidst our newfound capability to insert implants onto newly crystallized offspring, we have suddenly reached an impasse. Such augmentations would allow our young to form a natural bond with metallic minerals, unlocking their cybernetic potential from an early age and propelling our civilization to astonishing new heights.$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:373`

## cyber.320.root.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.root.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着将植入体安装进新嫩芽的全新技术问世，我们突然陷入了僵局。这样的强化改造将使我们的新一代通过菌丝网络传输电子脉冲，从很小的时候就释放出他们的义体潜力。$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

Amidst our newfound capability to insert cybernetic implants onto sproutlings, we have suddenly reached an impasse. These augmentations would allow our young to transmit electrical impulses through an enhanced network of hyphae, unlocking their cybernetic potential from an early age.$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:374`

## cyber.320.egg.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.egg.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着将植入体安装进幼雏的全新技术问世，我们突然陷入了僵局。这样的先进强化改造拥有将以科技永久重塑骨骼的能力，从很小的时候就释放出他们的义体潜力，并将我们的文明推向惊人的新高度。$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

Amidst our newfound capability to insert implants directly onto hatchlings, we have suddenly reached an impasse. These pioneering augmentations possess the power to forever transform their bond with technology, unlocking innate cybernetic potential from an early age and propelling our civilization to unprecedented heights.$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:375`

## cyber.320.other.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.other.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着将植入体安装进幼体的全新技术问世，我们也同时陷入了僵局：这样的先进强化改造拥有将以科技永久重塑骨骼的能力，从很小的时候就释放出他们的义体潜力，并将我们的文明推向惊人的新高度。$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

With the capability to insert cybernetic implants into infants, we also arrive at an impasse: these pioneering augmentations possess the power to forever transform our bond with technology, unlocking innate cybernetic potential from an early age and propelling our civilization to unprecedented heights.$cyber.320.end.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:376`

## cyber.320.end.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.320.end.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n然而，如果从这么小的时候就开始依赖于义体强化，可能会导致我们的物种最终失去在自然状态下生存的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

\n\nHowever, creating a reliance on cybernetic enhancement from such an early age could eventually result in our species losing the capability to survive in our natural state.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:377`

进化对我们是自然而然的事情。

### English

It is only natural for us to evolve.

### 中文

转化阵痛

### English

Conversion Pains

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:382`

## cyber.360.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.360.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，一些个体开始公开表达对接受实验性身体改造的懊悔。将脑干换成机械替代品会导致持久的生存恐惧，一些人将他们的经历描述为无法忍受的痛苦的持续循环。\n\n在不断要求赔偿的情况下，我们不得不澄清我们对国家责任的立场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Recently, a number of individuals have begun to publicly express remorse over their decision to undergo experimental body modifications. Exchanging the brainstem for a mechanical substitute has resulted in lasting existential dread, with some describing their experience as a continuous cycle of unbearable agony.\n\nAmidst ongoing demands for reparations, we are forced to clarify our position regarding the state's responsibility.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:383`

## cyber.405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

目睹了监管宽松的强化改造行业带来的利润，当地政府官员一直在争先恐后地跟上最新的发展。从资源开采到最终安装，控制植入体生产的每个阶段都要征收大幅税额。\n\n过分的官僚主义和高昂的费用已经减缓了我们的强化改造进度，阻碍了全面义体化的进程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

Witnessing the profits in the loosely regulated augmentation industry, local government officials have been scrambling to keep up with the latest developments. Broad taxes have been levied on every stage of cybernetic implant production, from resource extraction to final installation.\n\nThe additional bureaucracy and expense have slowed down our augmentation process, impeding progress toward full cyberization.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:393`

## cyber.410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

司法之战

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Judicial Warfare

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:400`

## cyber.410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于跟不上公众对控制植入体的采用，我们的立法者正在尽其所能限制某些方面的强化改造。\n\n最新提出的法规谴责我们“植入体成瘾”，每一次强化改造都“让我们离[Owner.Species.GetName]的定义越来越远”，并设定了一个每个人的强化体比例上限，只有在该限度之内才资格受到法律保护。毕竟，在某种程度上，我们有会变得更像机器而不是[Owner.Species.GetName]的风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Unable to keep up with the public's adoption of cybernetic implants, our lawmakers are doing everything they can to restrict certain aspects of augmentation.\n\nDecrying our "implant obsession," with each augmentation "taking us further away from what it means to be a [Owner.Species.GetName]," the latest proposed regulations set a maximum augmentation percentage per person allowed to still qualify for protection under the law. After all, at some point we risk becoming more machine than [Owner.Species.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:401`

### English

## cyber.415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对利润的狂热追逐中，强化改造市场充斥着廉价、低质的植入体，在这些设备不可避免地发生故障时，公司也拒绝提供客户支持。\n\n在一个最近广为报道的案例中，一位著名演员在舞台上表演时，心脏调节植入体发生了故障。不幸的是，结果是爆炸性的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

In the heady rush for profit, the augmentation market has been flooded by cheap, low-quality implants, with companies offering no customer support when these devices inevitably break down.\n\nIn one recent, well-publicized case, a popular performer's heart-modulating implant malfunctioned while on stage. The result was, unfortunately, explosive.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:408`

## cyber.420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.420.name&lang=en)

### 中文

危险设计

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

Dangerous by Design

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:416`

## cyber.420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，一种引人疑虑的新型义体植入体得到了广泛的关注。这些植入体的设计不是为了帮助使用者，而是为了伤害他人：这些毒素喷雾器、纳米绞杀器和电子大脑干扰器席卷了大街小巷。\n\n支持者认为，这些植入体对于自卫是必要的，而其他人则坚持认为，这种技术不可能带来任何好处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Recently, a dubious new variety of cybernetic implant has been gaining traction. These implants are not designed to help the user, but rather to injure others: toxin sprayers, nano garrottes, and cyberbrain scramblers flood our streets.\n\nProponents argue that these implants are necessary for self defense, while others insist no good can possibly come of such technology.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:417`

## cyber.425.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.425.name&lang=en)

### 中文

控制软件攻击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Control Software Hack

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:424`

## cyber.425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一次协同攻击中，多个黑客组织协力盗取了驱动所有植入体的核心软件。他们威胁称，如果我们不答应他们的要求，就要把代码公布于众。\n\n到目前为止，我们没有理由怀疑他们的决心。如果代码被公开，我们将别无选择，只能进行重写，这种延迟将对我们的强化改造工作造成毁灭性的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

In a coordinated attack, multiple hacker groups have gained access to the core software that powers all cybernetic implants. They are threatening to release the code to the public unless we give in to their demands.\n\nThus far, we have no reason to doubt their resolve. Should the code be made public, we will have no choice but to rewrite it, a delay that will have a devastating impact on our augmentation efforts.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:425`

### English

Undependable Augments

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:432`

## cyber.430.desc.demands

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.430.desc.demands&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们向入侵安全系统的组织支付了巨额赎金，但昨晚我们的主要代码库还是被上传到了所有公共信息网络。泄露的内容开头还附带一张淫秽图片，嘲笑我们无力阻止攻击。\n\n公众舆论逐渐发酵，对控制植入体的“买家懊悔”明显增加了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

Despite paying a massive ransom to the groups responsible for the security breach, late last night our primary code base was uploaded to on every public information network. The leak was preceded by an obscene graphic, mocking our inability to prevent the attacks.\n\nPublic discourse has already turned bitter, with a noticeable increase in 'buyer's remorse' for cybernetic implants.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:433`

## cyber.430.desc.protest

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.430.desc.protest&lang=en)

### 中文

重写植入体控制程序费时费力，减慢了产出的节奏。\n\n由于民众越来越依赖于最新的强化体，许多人走上街头抗议产品的延期发售。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

Rewriting the implant control software has taken time and effort, and slowed down production.\n\nThe population has become ever more dependent on the latest augments, and many have taken to the streets to protest delayed product launches.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:434`

## cyber.21.name

### 中文

强化人口

### English

An Augmented Population

## cyber.21.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.21.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天是我们社会的一个转折点。我们终于得以用全内置机械部件来强化改造自己的躯体。\n\n这个过程并非一帆风顺，转变远非易事。但无论付出了什么代价，现在的新躯体已经将无尽的可能性展开在了我们的面前。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Today marks a turning point for our society. We are now able to augment our bodies with fully-integrated machine parts.\n\nThe process was not without difficulty, the transition far from easy. But whatever may have been lost along the way, our new existence offers endless possibilities.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:441`

## futureshock_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afutureshock_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不安于自然所给予的现状，努力地超越了有机躯体的极限。全面铺开的植入体应用只遭遇了微不足道的问题，并且都已逐一迎刃而解。绝大多数人口为全新的义体所带来的一切感到欣喜若狂。\n\n我们正站在成就伟大的大门前，未来可期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

We refuse to settle for what nature provided, and instead strive to exceed the limits of the organic body. Our full-scale distribution of implants has encountered only minor issues, all of which can be easily resolved. The vast majority of the population is exhilarated by what their new cybernetics offer.\n\nWe truly stand on the precipice of greatness. What happens next remains to be seen.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:453`

## auth_cyber_democratic_individualist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_democratic_individualist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

高度先进的神经安全措施让公民可以完全、实时、直接地参与决定政策的投票。\n\n这种民主政府定期举行选举，有公民都可以投票选出代表他们的议员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

Highly advanced neurosecurity protocols allow citizens to vote on policy decisions in real-time directly.\n\nThis democratic government holds regular elections where all citizens can vote on who should represent them.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:465`

## auth_cyber_democratic_collectivist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_democratic_collectivist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

意识交互界面可以促成全义体人之间实时的传感器数据、情感响应和思维模式共享。为了防止发生强烈的外体响应敏感性，情感防火墙会过滤掉对共享情感数据包的不必要反应。\n\n这种民主政府定期举行选举，有公民都可以投票选出代表他们的议员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

Consciousness interfaces allow real-time sharing of sensory data, emotional responses, and thought patterns between full cyborgs. As a safeguard against the sensitivity of intense extra-body responses, emotional firewalls filter out unwanted responses to shared emotive data packets.\n\nThis democratic government holds regular elections where all citizens can vote on who should represent them.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:473`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

$auth_democratic_election_tt$\n£pop£ §HCyborg Pops§! are not affected by Happiness\n£pop£ §HCyborg Pops§! join the §YInterlink§! faction\n$unlock_edict_self_preservation_override$

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:474`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_oligarchic_collectivist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近湿件的进步使得控制睡眠成为可能。受试对象的神经通路与服务器相连，服务器向大脑输入计算任务，或命令他们的身体执行重复性工作。这就是字面意义上的“梦中的工作”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Recent wetware advancements have made it possible to control people in their sleep. The subject's neural pathways are connected to servers, which feed the brain computational tasks or command their body to perform repetitive work. A real dream job.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:497`

## auth_cyber_dictatorial_individualist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_dictatorial_individualist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

强制植入为我们提供了强有力的控制和执法的工具，加强了政权对其臣民的控制。这种俗称为“独裁芯片”的植入体可以监视它们的宿主，并在出现问题时自动向当局发出警报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

Mandatory implantation provides robust control and law enforcement tools, strengthening the regime's grip over its subjects. The colloquially named "dictator-chips" monitor their owners and alert the authorities automatically if trouble arises.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:520`

## auth_cyber_dictatorial_collectivist_desc

### 中文

独裁者的个人崇拜和对臣民的影响力通过控制植入物得到了增强。敬畏领袖的人民不太可能反抗政权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

The dictator's cult of personality and reach over their subjects have been empowered via cybernetic implants. A people in awe of their leader are unlikely to rebel against the regime.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:528`

### English

## auth_cyber_imperial_individualist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_imperial_individualist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

皇位的指定继承人所继承的不仅仅是皇位，还有代代相传的专属植入体。其中包含了祖祖辈辈积累下来的无价知识财富，传授如何治理国家的各种智慧。相同的技术也以一种较小的形式在整个帝国中使用，其他人也能类似地传承经验。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

The designated successor of the imperial throne inherits not only the position but a specialized implant passed through generations. It contains a wealth of priceless knowledge from their predecessors, imparting all types of wisdom on how to govern. The same technology is used throughout the empire in a lesser form, allowing other individuals to pass on their experiences onwards.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:565`

## auth_cyber_imperial_collectivist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_imperial_collectivist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国网络可以让所有人口访问一种全新的大型娱乐设施。可以在首都建造一座一座雄伟的$building_cyberdome$以容纳强化运动员参与最血性的义体运动，并将其广播向许许多多的星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

The imperial network allows the entire population to access whole new avenues of mass-entertainment. A massive $building_cyberdome$ can be built to the capital to host ferocious cybersports with augmented athletes and broadcast this across multiple worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:588`

## building_cyberdome_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_cyberdome_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

举办强化改造运动员之间野蛮的义体竞技以娱乐帝国公民。增强的角斗士、运动员和短跑运动员为观众的青睐互相角逐，直到受伤或死亡为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

Hosts savage cybersports with augmented athletes for the entertainment of the imperial citizens. Enhanced gladiators, athletes and sprinters compete for our favor until damaged or dead.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:594`

## auth_cyber_corporate_individualist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_corporate_individualist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

广告牌和手持设备等过时的工具无法完全吸引消费者的注意力，而现在我们可以扫描潜在客户的思想，将特定的信息和强调的行动呼吁直接发送到个人的大脑中。\n\n这一创新也使我们能够监控恒星基地的访客是否有海盗活动，但也可能会产生对黑市信号屏蔽器的需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Outmoded tools such as billboards and hand-held devices have failed to fully capture a consumer's attention. We can now scan the minds of potential customers and beam specific messaging and emphatic calls-to-action directly into an individual brain.\n\nThis innovation also allows us to monitor visitors across our starbases for pirate activity, but is likely to create a demand for black market signal blockers.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:604`

### 中文

改良企业

### English

Corporate Kaizen

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:611`

## auth_cyber_corporate_collectivist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aauth_cyber_corporate_collectivist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改良，是一种积极进取的态度，是一种现代软件和湿件环境下的坚实哲学。多元化的升级选择为我们的客户群提供了个人成就和社会协作的手段。\n\n其中也留了一个用于监控所有连接到恒星基地网络用户的后门。然而，强化体的巨大利润空间可能会创造一个地下转售市场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

Kaizen, the will to continuously improve, is a sound philosophy for the modern software and wetware environment. An extensive selection of upgrades affords our customer base individual achievement and the means towards social synergy.\n\nThey also contain a backdoor for monitoring all users connected to starbase networks. However, the augments' substantial profit margins are likely to create an underground resale market.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:612`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_battle_royale_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

激活一个游戏化的配额界面，吸引专家和劳工竞争晋升和绩效徽章。这种无休止的折磨部分抵消了生产率的提高。

寡头政治点明了当前被剥削派系艰苦卓绝的努力，提升了他们的支持度和吸引力。这种短暂的美名也为社会流动打开了一扇大门。

## edict_neurocasted_spectacles_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_neurocasted_spectacles_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从将首都的义体斗兽场中残酷的赛事画面直接通过神经广播给义体大脑以来，人们纷纷涌向首都。观众们可以安全地身处于荚舱中，以第一人称的方式欣赏角斗士的体验。这是比赛之年！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

By neurocasting the brutal sport spectacles of the capital's Cyberdome directly to the cyberbrain, people flock to the capital. With their bodies safe in pods, spectators share the gladiators' experiences in first-person. A year of games!

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:662`

## edict_customer_insights_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_customer_insights_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

广泛的网络调研和实时数据分析让我们可以对市场的动向以前所未有的速度进行评估和调整。资源被转移到具有最高利润潜力的功能和产品上。

### English

Extensively networked surveys and real-time data analysis enable us to gauge and react to market shifts faster than ever before. Resources are diverted to features and products with the highest profit potential.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

未来震撼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

Future Shock

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:687`

## cyber.505.desc.gov

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.505.desc.gov&lang=en)

### 中文

对我们人民的强化改造是一场流水线生产流程和决策指定的重大胜利。众多不同的声音得到了倾听，他们所关切的问题也得到了解决。如今，每一名§Y[root.GetSpeciesAdj]§!都有平等的机会享受这些改变一生的植入体。\n\n合乎逻辑的下一步是对这项技术的更广泛影响进行研究。我们是利用它来推动民主进程，还是通过经验同步来提升我们的劳工？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

The augmentation of our people has been a triumph of streamlined manufacturing processes and decision-making. Our multitude of distinct voices have been acknowledged with their concerns addressed. Now, every single §Y[root.GetSpeciesAdj]§! has equal opportunity to benefit from these life-altering implants.\n\nThe logical next step is to examine the wider implications of this technology. Are we to leverage it for the democratic process or elevate our workers via experience synchronization?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:688`

## cyber.505.desc.cor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.505.desc.cor&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与合作公司的强化改造流程已经成功完成。植入体以惊人的效率制造、分发和安装。我们听取了§Y[root.GetSpeciesAdj]§!社会的许多意见，他们的合理关切也得到了处理。我们已经准备好了以全新的活力面对未来。\n\n政府现在准备采取下一步行动。我们可以利用技术进步来改善民主进程，或者为劳动者进行植入体同步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

The augmentation process with our partnered corporations has been successfully completed. Implants have been manufactured, distributed, and installed with staggering efficiency. The many voices of §Y[root.GetSpeciesAdj]§! society have been heard, and their legitimate concerns have been dealt with. We are ready to face the future with renewed vigor.\n\nThe government is now prepared to take its own next step. We can use our technological advances to improve the democratic process or synchronize implants in the service of workers.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:689`

## cyber.505.desc.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.505.desc.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管政府官员有些担心，但植入体的去中心化分发模式还是奏效了。小诊所和服务机构如雨后春笋般出现在大街小巷，让人们无需长途跋涉就能完成自身的强化改造。\n\n我们的政府也必须做好继续前进的准备。我们可以利用技术进步来改善民主流程，或是进行经验同步，为自己与劳动者们造福。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

Despite some apprehension from government officials, the decentralized distribution model of the implants has worked. Small clinics and services are popping up on every street corner allowing anyone to augment themselves without having to travel great distances.\n\nOur government must be ready to move on as well. We can either use the implants to strengthen our democratic process or use them to synchronize our experiences for the benefits of ourselves and the workers.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:690`

## cyber.510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

权力结构重构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

Power Restructured

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:697`

## cyber.510.desc.gov

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.510.desc.gov&lang=en)

### 中文

这种里程碑式的转变只能由社会上层的来理解和实施，以免在我们的臣民中产生混乱和分歧。当§Y[Species.GetNamePlural]§!被安装上政府分发的义体植入体时，我们获得了一个绝无仅有的探索这些芯片提供全新可能性的机会。\n\n我们应该为社会创造激烈的竞争，让我们的工人提升到全新的高度，还是应该利用起睡眠中闲置的计算能力呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

This monumental shift can only be understood and enacted by the upper echelon of society, lest it leads to confusion and disagreements within our subjects. While the §Y[Species.GetNamePlural]§! are fitted with their government-issued cybernetic implants, and we have a unique opportunity to explore untapped opportunities these chips now afford.\n\nShould we push for a society where fierce competition raises our workers to new heights or harness the idle computational power of the sleeping minds?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:698`

## cyber.510.desc.cor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.510.desc.cor&lang=en)

### 中文

随着政府批准的公司开始大量生产不断改进的生物芯片，我们必须决定以何种方式来利用好这一次令人难以置信的社会变革。\n\n我们是应该模仿优胜劣汰的自然选择，还是发掘沉睡中的大脑尚未开发的潜力?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

As government-sanctioned corporations churn out ever-improving biochips, we must make a decision on how to best utilize this incredible societal change.\n\nShould we mimic nature, where only the strongest survive, or nurture the untapped potential of the slumbering brain?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:699`

## cyber.510.desc.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.510.desc.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

不管是好是坏，人们都将有机会通过制造和分销的义体强化硬件来证明自己，让我们可以自由地考虑大局，并决定如何最大限度地利用这种不可预见的社会转变。\n\n我们是应该促使人们完成更高的生产配额，还是让他们在睡梦中为我们工作？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

For better or worse, the people will be allowed to prove themselves in manufacturing and distributing the cyber-augmentation hardware, freeing us to consider the big picture and how to make the most of this unforeseen societal shift.\n\nShould we push people to meet ever higher quotas, or instead, make them work for us in their sleep?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:700`

## cyber.515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

人群控制

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

Crowd Controlled

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:707`

## cyber.515.desc.gov

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.515.desc.gov&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们的强化改造计划中出现了许多提升稳定性和控制力的机遇。在我们为政府生产植入体并在人口中广泛地安装的过程中，秘密地嵌入可以引导人口实现更高生产力的算法可谓小菜一碟。\n\n我们可以通过在个人植入体中添加监控和后门程序来增加监视能力，或者我们可以安装代码，让我们§Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!光芒四射的人格影响大众。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

Several opportunities to increase stability and control have appeared within our augmentation program. As we manufacture government-issued implants and install them across our population, secretly installing algorithms to guide the population toward a preferred outcome would be easy.\n\nWe could increase our surveillance coverage by adding monitoring and backdoor tools to personal implants, or we could install code that allows our §Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!'s radiant personality to influence the masses.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:708`

## cyber.515.desc.cor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.515.desc.cor&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的合作伙伴公司已经准备好在社会范围内制造、分发和安装义体植入体，而即将到来的固件更新为我们提供了一个独特的机会。\n\n我们可以在个人强化硬件中秘密嵌入监控工具，这样就能找出有问题的人，或者可以创造一个人格覆写函数，将§Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!的意志烙印在大众的心中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

Our partner corporations are ready to manufacture, distribute, and install cybernetic implants on a societal scale, and an upcoming firmware update provides us with a unique opportunity.\n\nWe could include hidden surveillance tools to personal augmentation hardware, allowing us to root out problematic individuals. Alternatively, we can create a personality override function that imprints the will of our §Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§! the masses.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:709`

## cyber.515.desc.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.515.desc.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

综合医院、街角商店和骇客群体现在正准备分发并在大众身上安装义体植入体。但是，没有必要把我们的权力如此完全地分散到这项令人兴奋的新技术上。\n\n我们可以提供客订化的植入体修改，把秘密监视工具隐藏其中，以对有问题的个人进行密切关注，或者我们可以安装人格覆盖算法，让我们§Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!的意志在潜意识中引导人民走向伟大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

General hospitals, corner shops, and hacker communities are now ready to distribute and install cybernetic implants on the populace. But there is no need to decentralize our power over this new and exciting technology so wholly.\n\nWe could provide custom implant modifications, with our own covert surveillance tools hidden within, to be able to keep tabs on problematic individuals. Alternatively, we could install personality-overriding algorithms that will allow the will of our §Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§! to subconsciously guide our people towards greatness.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:710`

在个人植入体中藏入监视工具。

### English

Hide surveillance tools in personal implants.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:711`

### 中文

未来震撼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

Future Shock

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:717`

## cyber.520.desc.gov

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.520.desc.gov&lang=en)

### 中文

政府已有效地调配了庞大的资源来进行人口的强化。政府批准的强化改造中心日夜不停地安装，并指导人们如何正确地使用、维护植入体。\n\n现在的问题是，植入体的实际价值是应该集中在增强我们已经令人印象深刻的领导力上，还是作为下属娱乐和分散注意力的一种手段。\n\n为我们的皇室制造最高质量的植入体可以让我们的领导层得以在代际保存知识，不可估量地提高稳定性。或者帝国可以提升首都的地位，让义体强化的运动员在这里参与最极端的体育盛事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:644`

### English

The government has deployed its vast resources to augment the population efficiently. Sanctioned augmentation centers have been installing implants day and night, and people have been instructed on proper usage and maintenance practices.\n\nThe question is whether the actual value of implants should be focused on enhancing our already impressive leadership or as a means to entertain and distract those who work under them.\n\nHaving the highest quality implants available to our dynasty could allow our leadership to preserve knowledge between generations, providing immeasurable stability. Or, the empire can elevate the capital and entertain with cybernetically enhanced athletes participating in state-of-the-art bloodsport events.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:718`

## cyber.520.desc.cor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.520.desc.cor&lang=en)

### 中文

官方批准的公司正在执行着操作指令。所有的人口都以义体植入体进行了强化，并准备好了以前所未有的效率提供服务。\n\n现在的问题是应该将植入体的实际价值集中在哪里。\n\n为我们的皇室制造最高质量的植入体可以让我们的领导层得以在代际保存知识，不可估量地提高稳定性。或者帝国可以提升首都的地位，让义体强化的运动员在这里参与最极端的体育盛事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

Officially sanctioned corporations have performed within operational directives. The entire population has been augmented with cybernetic implants and is ready to serve more efficiently than ever before.\n\nThe question now is where the actual value of implants should be focused on.\n\nHaving the highest quality implants available to our dynasty could allow our leadership to preserve knowledge between generations, providing immeasurable stability. Or, the empire can elevate the capital and entertain with cybernetically enhanced athletes participating in state-of-the-art bloodsport events.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:719`

## cyber.520.desc.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.520.desc.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管有许多帝国官员持保留意见，但义体植入体的去中心化分配安装已经取得了成功。各个星球上的小诊所一直在不知疲倦地努力强化改造我们的人口。\n\n现在的问题是植入体的真正价值应该集中在什么方面。我们可以让统治者的知识储存在代际植入体中，或者为大众提供前所未见的义体人体育盛事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

Despite numerous reservations from imperial officials, decentralized distribution and installation of cybernetic implants has been successful. Multiple small clinics across our worlds have been working tirelessly to enhance our population.\n\nThe question now is where the actual value of implants should be focused. This could mean preserving imperial knowledge within intergenerational implants or providing the masses with cyborg bloodsport, the likes that have never before been viewed by any ocular implant.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:720`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.525.name&lang=en)

### 中文

植入体入股

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

Implants Incorporated

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:727`

## cyber.525.desc.gov

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.525.desc.gov&lang=en)

### 中文

庞大而徒劳的政府植入体计划即将完成，这为我们带来了进一步扩大与这一前所未有的产品发布相关的收入来源的机会。我们的分析团队提出了两种潜在的收入来源，两者都有各自的风险和回报。\n\n政府是否应该要求在未来所有的视觉植入体中包含广告，还是要求对强化人口进行定期升级?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

The expansive yet ineffectual government implant program nears completion, presenting us with an opportunity to further expand revenue sources related to this unprecedented product launch. Our analytics team is proposing two potential sources of income, both with their own risks and rewards.\n\nShould the government require the inclusion of advertising in all future ocular implants, or mandate regular upgrades for the augmented population?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:728`

## cyber.525.desc.cor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.525.desc.cor&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于我们高度优化的企业基础设施，植入体的生产和安装与预期一样高效。这些新型植入体实际上带来了无穷无尽的商机。\n\n其中有两条特别有前途的途径。虽然相互排斥，但两种方案的利润率都很高。\n\n问题是，我们应该要求在未来所有的视觉植入体中包含广告，还是要求对强化人口进行定期升级?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

Implant production and installation is as efficient as expected from our highly optimized corporate infrastructure. The business opportunities of these new implants are practically endless.\n\nTwo particularly promising avenues present themselves. While mutually exclusive, both predict stellar profit margins.\n\nThe question is, should we require the inclusion of advertising in all future ocular implants, or mandate regular upgrades for the augmented population?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:729`

## cyber.525.desc.ind

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.525.desc.ind&lang=en)

### 中文

不出所料，义体件杂乱无章的“公共”植入正走向一个仁慈的结尾。我们现在还有机会影响这项有前途的新技术的发展方向，并在此过程中提高我们的产品利润率。\n\n应该要求在未来所有的视觉植入体中包含广告，还是要求对强化人口进行定期升级?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

Unsurprisingly, the haphazard "communal" implementation of cyberware is coming to its merciful end. We now have the opportunity to influence the direction of this promising new technology, raising our product margins in the process.\n\nShould advertisements be included in all future ocular implants, or would it be better to mandate a continuous upgrade cycle for software and wetware?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:730`

Prototype ad-supported ocular implants.

## cyber.610.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.610.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一种非法植入模组正在员工中广为传播，它可以刺激大脑产生任意的神经递质。这将会有广泛的用途，例如灌输强烈的快乐或者增强疼痛耐受性。从这些非法植入物中逆向工程出一个稳定的版本需要大量的投资，而且这种刺激的效果必将逐渐减退。\n\n我们是应该将这种非法植入体重新包装为商业化的版本，还是仅仅对其使用进行规范？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

An underground implant mod is spreading amongst the workforce that stimulates the brain to produce neurotransmitter chemicals at will. This has a wide array of uses from instilling intense pleasure to increasing pain tolerance. Reverse-engineering a stable version out of the illegal implant would require substantial investments, and the stimulating effects are bound to wear off eventually.\n\nShould we create a commercial version, repackage the underground prototype, or merely regulate their use?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:738`

## cyber.615.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.615.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工作生活分离

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

Work-Life Divide

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:743`

## cyber.615.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.615.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

越来越多的公司要求在工作期间的所有经验必须储存在义体大脑中的特殊区域，在工作以外的时候不得访问。\n\n他们声称，这种功能只会在高度机密的任务中使用，以避免商业窃密。有一些甚至要求雇员在工作中屏蔽个人经验，以避免个人道德对生产力造成干扰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

More and more companies require that all experiences during office hours are stored in a cyberbrain memory area inaccessible outside work.\n\nThey claim to use this functionality only with highly confidential tasks to prevent corporate espionage. Some even require employees to wall off their private experiences at work to prevent personal ethics interfering with productivity.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:744`

### English

### English

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:746`

### 中文

付费订阅

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

Subscription Based Monetization

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:749`

## cyber.625.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.625.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着义体件在我们的生活中愈发根深蒂固，盈利模式的变化将极大地影响整个社会。近来，植入体制造商将一些基本功能限制在订阅模式中成为了一种潮流。如果忘记付款，义体躯干可能会突然被锁定、视力可能会消失。消费者权益组织正在敦促我们出台措施阻止这种现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

With cyberware being so engrained in our lives, changes in monetization models affect our entire society dramatically. The recent trend has implants manufacturers lock essential functionalities behind a subscription model. Miss a payment and suddenly, your cyberlimbs lock up, and your optics turn off. Consumer organizations are urging us to put a stop to these practices.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:750`

### English

## cyber.655.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.655.name&lang=en)

### 中文

难以预料的机械故障

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Unforeseeable Mechanical Failure

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:762`

## cyber.655.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.655.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

义体件工程师为我们想出了一种全新的商业机遇。只要稍微降低植入体的耐用度，我们就可以利用用户替换必要部件的刚需来显著地提升利润。\n\n通过在日常磨损最频繁的部分使用劣质生物合金，植入体会以对消费者伤害最小的方式发生故障，从而制造更换需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Our cyberware engineers have come to us with an economic opportunity. With only a slight reduction of implant durability, we could generate a substantial increase in profits as users will have to replace essential parts.\n\nBy using an inferior bio-alloy in the sections experiencing most of the usual wear and tear the implants should fail in a manner that minimizes harm to the consumer, but require replacement.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:763`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

Parental Controls

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:784`

## cyber.805.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.805.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监护人希望能通过幼体大脑中的植入体对他们的孩子进行更多的控制。对运动功能的接管目前仅限于警方使用，但有些人希望把这种权力也授予孩子的监护人。\n\n尽管这很明显违背了身体自主权，但许多其他方式的家长控制已经得到允许。替代方案是允许一些有限的控制，例如让行为不良的孩子减慢速度或降低音量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

With brain implants installed in infants, caretakers are asking for more control over their children. The ability to take over motor functions is currently reserved for enforcers, but some want to extend these rights to parental guardians as well.\n\nWhile this is a clear violation of bodily autonomy, several other forms of parental coercion are already permitted. The alternative would be to permit limited control, such as slowing down or reducing the volume level of the misbehaving child.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:785`

## cyber.810.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.810.name&lang=en)

### 中文

知识传输

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Knowledge Transfer

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:790`

## cyber.810.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.810.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在义体大脑植入体方面的发展给予了我们一个诱人的机遇。比起又主观又缓慢的教学方法，我们可以将关键的知识直接在大脑之间传输。该过程需要无菌的实验室环境，因此无法进行大规模应用。接收到的信息也会部分覆盖掉接收者的同类别记忆。\n\n最有影响的应用方式包括在科学家之间共享知识。这可以抵消来自义体化过程的一些工程学研究负担，或者将重点放在其他领域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

The development of the cybernetic brain implants gives us an enticing opportunity. Instead of relying on subjective and slow teaching methods, we could transfer key knowledge directly from one brain to another. This requires sterile laboratory conditions, and thus would not be exploitable in large scale. The received information also overwrites parts of the recipient's own memories of a similar kind.\n\nThe most impactful application would involve knowledge sharing between scientists. This could offset some of the engineering debt from the cyberization process or be focused on other areas.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:791`

### English

## cyber.815.name

### 中文

离散化的爱

### English

Decentralized Love

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:797`

## cyber.815.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.815.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在许多[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]愉快地用现成的义体来强化日常生活和人际关系的同时，越来越多的多配偶家庭转而使用带有定制硬件的私人网络。这些家庭群体选择生活在量身定制的隔离中，以独特的亲密感分享彼此的感受和经历。\n\n有社会福利团体和学者称赞这种现象，认为这种对人际关系和家庭纽带的客订化强化改造可以造福所有人。但也有人对这样的避世隐居产生了担忧，甚至认为这种行为可能导致个体在这种迷你的“爱情完形”中迷失自我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

Whereas many [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have gladly augmented their everyday lives and relationships using off-the shelf cybernetics, a growing number of multiple-partner households in particular have instead taken to private networks with bespoke hardware. These domestic groups have chosen to live in tailor-made isolation, sharing each other's feelings and experiences with unique intimacy.\n\nSome social welfare groups and scholars applaud this phenomenon, suggesting everyone could benefit from custom augmentation of relationships and family bonds. Others have raised concerns about such a retreat from wider society, with some even suggesting the practice may cause individuals to lose themselves in miniaturized 'love gestalts'.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:798`

## cyber.820.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.820.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在整个[root.GetSpeciesAdj]历史上，外部亚文化一直如影随形。随着义体增强技术的出现，情况发生了变化：一些边缘群体开始对自己进行大刀阔斧的改造，以至于看起来根本不像是[root.GetSpeciesAdj]。尤其是年轻人，他们对高度定制的[root.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]、3D躯体艺术和其他装饰性附肢的渴望正在拉大代沟。\n\n支持者声称，他们正在拥抱更真实的自我。他们通过放弃[root.GetSpeciesAdj]的同质化维护自己生命的主观能动性。但越来越多的家长和社会专家反对这种观点，他们谴责这些人是要破坏我们社会的恶棍。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

Outsider subcultures have existed throughout [root.GetSpeciesAdj] history, but with the advent of cybernetic enhancements the situation has changed: fringe groups have begun altering themselves so radically as to appear non-[root.GetSpeciesAdj]. Youths in particular are straining generation gaps with their lust for heavily customized [root.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural], 3D body art, and other decorative appendages.\n\nProponents claim they are embracing their truer selves, and that by renouncing [root.GetSpeciesAdj] homogeneity they are simply asserting agency over their own lives. These views are not shared by a growing number of concerned parents and social pundits, who denounce such individuals as miscreants out to ruin our society.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:808`

## cyber.821.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.821.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成千上万的年轻人、活动家和艺术家聚集在一起，呼吁义体强化改造自由。抗议者有的从外表上看来是正常的[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，也有的拥有定制肢体、重塑躯体，还有的看起来像是外星人。他们都在声援那些因在义体装饰方面的个人选择而面临歧视的[root.GetAdj]公民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

Concerned youths, activists, and artists have gathered in their thousands to demand freedom of cybernetic augmentation. Protestors run the gamut from outwardly typical [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] to individuals with custom limbs, reshaped torsos, and other alien-looking forms. All proclaim solidarity with [root.GetAdj] citizens who face discrimination for their personal choices in cybernetic adornment.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:818`

## cyber.822.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.822.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

医疗工作者抱怨称，目前治疗那些使用着极端特殊义体强化体的病人时，面临着前所未有的奇怪困难。\n\n这些精心雕刻的个体即使只是一些小毛病，也大多需要使用特殊的治疗措施，如定制工具或剧烈拆卸等。此类干预措施的费用甚至超过了那些未修饰的非[root.GetSpeciesAdj]患者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Medical workers have raised complaints about the bizarre and unprecedented obstacles they now face when treating patients with radically unique cybernetic augments.\n\nMany of these elaborately-sculpted individuals require special treatment measures such as custom tools or drastic disassembly, even for minor ailments. The costs for such interventions far exceed those associated with unmodified, non-[root.GetSpeciesAdj] patients.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:829`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.822.a&lang=en)

这种成本是合理的。

### English

The costs are justified.

### English

## cyber.830.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.830.name&lang=en)

义体风湿

## cyber.830.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.830.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有一部分强化人口汇报称暴露在寒冷天气时会感受到疼痛。这种状况在§H[cold_planet.GetName]§!上格外严重，因为刺骨的严寒在那如同家常便饭一般。\n\n已知金属植入体在低温下确实令人不太舒服，但不至于产生这样的严重反应。这一情况影响到了我们的劳动力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

A segment of our augmented population reports feeling pain when exposed to cold weather. The situation is particularly serious on §H[cold_planet.GetName]§!, where freezing temperatures are the norm.\n\nMetal implants are known to be uncomfortable in low temperatures, but should not be causing a reaction this severe. The situation is impacting our workforce.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:835`

### English

## COLD_METAL_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACOLD_METAL_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在寒冷环境居住的人口汇报了各种与义体植入体有关的疼痛症状。通过临床实验可以让我们获得更多数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

Populations living in freezing climates report cases of severe pains related to cybernetic implants. A clinical trial will allow us to gather more data.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:840`

## cyber.835.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.835.name&lang=en)

### 中文

医学报告

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Medical Report

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:842`

## cyber.835.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.835.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

临床实验无法确定义体件暴露于寒冷天气与受影响患者的年龄、性别、职业和植入体类型之间的明确相关性。\n\n有些医学专家认为这种疼痛是精神性的，治疗应该集中在止痛和行为疗法上。其他人则敦促我们不要低估这个问题，应该资助一个项目来推进治疗方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

Clinical trials could not establish a definitive correlation between the exposure of cyberware to cold weather and the age, sex, occupation and type of implants used by the affected patients.\n\nSome medical professionals argue the pain is psychosomatic, and treatment should be limited to painkillers and behavioral therapy. Others urge us to not underestimate the issue, and instead to fund a program to advance treatment options.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:843`

### English

Their pain is all in their heads. 

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:844`

## cyber.840.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.840.name&lang=en)

### 中文

我心何向

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

Where Our Hearts Will Take Us

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:849`

## cyber.840.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.840.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的民族自古以来都是旅人、牧民和流浪者。有些人的旅行是到达目的地的一种手段，而我们的目的地就是旅行本身。身体强化改造使新的星球更加触手可及，但也使我们能够跟踪到那些太过离经叛道的人。就是说，并非所有人都对这种变化感到满意。\n\n越来越多的人开始篡改自己的植入体，通过脱离网络以保护自己的隐私。目前这仅仅是一个小众趋势，但它可能会带来麻烦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

Our people have always been travelers. Nomads. Wanderers. While for some traveling is a means to reach a destination, for us the journey is a destination in itself. Body augmentation has made it easier to reach new worlds, but has also allowed us to track those who may stray too far. Not everyone is happy with this change.\n\nAn increasing number of people have been tampering with their implants, dropping themselves off the grid to preserve their privacy. For now it's a niche trend, but it could create trouble.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:850`

## cyber.840.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.840.a&lang=en)
