他们没有错。禁止追踪科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

They're right. Ban tracking technology.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:851`

## cyber.855.intro.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.855.intro.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

认知微处理器、增强的人工智能家教、促进神经可塑性再生的设计酶——这些都还只是为学龄前学生准备的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

Cognitive micro-processors, enhanced A.I. tutors, designer enzymes to promote neuroplastic regeneration - and all of this just for preschool students.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:862`

## cyber.855.gov.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.855.gov.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.855.intro.desc$\n\n跟上科技发展的步伐让我们的国有教育计划背上了沉重的负担。然而那些只能依赖公共强化体的人和那些能享用最尖端科技的有钱人家之间的差距还在越变越大。\n\n维持一个公平的制度将需要投入大量资源进行一项为期五年的计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

$cyber.855.intro.desc$\n\nKeeping pace with technological change has placed a heavy burden on our state-sponsored education programs. The performance gap is widening between those who rely on public augments and households that can afford cutting-edge technology.\n\nMaintaining an equitable system will require a significant, five-year investment of resources.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:863`

## cyber.855.corp.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.855.corp.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.855.intro.desc$\n\n好的教育可以相当昂贵，而许多人感觉越来越难以跟上脚步了。然而那些能享用最尖端强化改造的有钱人家和只能依赖二手或者仿冒模块的人之间的差距还在越变越大。\n\n为了避免进一步的社会分层，有必要对我们的教育系统进行一项重大的五年投资。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

$cyber.855.intro.desc$\n\nA good education can be expensive, and many are finding it increasingly difficult to keep pace. The performance gap is widening between households that can afford cutting edge augmentation and those that must rely on older, hand-me-down, or knock-off models.\n\nTo prevent further social stratification, it will be necessary to make a significant, five-year investment in our education system.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:864`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:778`

## cyber.860.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.860.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在教育强化件方面的投资取得了可观的回报。\n\n开放公众对高质量义体的获取创造了一种真正的学术精英统治，在这种秩序下，每个人——无论其社会阶层——都能充分发挥自己的潜力。这些好处在科学领域尤为明显，科研产出和创新水平全面提高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

Our investment in educational augmentation has paid off handsomely.\n\nPublic access to high-quality cybernetics has created a true academic meritocracy in which everyone - no matter their social class - can achieve their full potential. The benefits are particularly noticeable in the sciences, where research output and innovation have improved across the board.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:871`

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:875`

## cyber.865.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.865.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事实证明，不动用公共资金去资助教育强化件的决定是明智的。\n\n我们的社会崇尚个人卓越。尽管对不断扩大的“社会鸿沟”的批评不绝于耳，但从学术竞争中却脱颖而出了一些全星系受教育程度最高、最具生产力的领袖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

The decision to refrain from publicly funding educational augmentation has proven a wise one.\n\nOurs is a society that believes in individual excellence. Despite scattered criticism about the widening 'social divide', academic competition has resulted in some of the most highly-educated and productive leaders in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:876`

### English

Limit Breaker

## cyber.1005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“[Species.GetName]记录再突破”这样的标题在[Owner.GetAdjective]的体育新闻中已经屡见不鲜了。随着[GetAthletePlural]们竞相使用更新、更复杂的改装来超越彼此，运动能力的极限也在不断被突破。\n\n“强化体育”的粉丝们为这些结果欣喜若狂，但越来越多的铁杆爱好者呼吁应该单独设立强化运动员的项目。还有一些人希望限制参加团体比赛的改造人的数量，让纯天然的[GetAthletePlural]在聚光灯下还能占有一席之地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

'Another [Species.GetName] Record Broken' - this headline has become commonplace in [Owner.GetAdjective] sporting life. As [GetAthletePlural] race to outdo one another with newer and more sophisticated modifications, the limits of athletic performance are constantly being pushed.\n\nWhile fans of 'enhanced athletics' are ecstatic with the results, a growing number of hardcore devotees have called for enhanced individuals to be confined to a separate division. A final group instead wants to limit the amount of modified who compete in team events meaning that natural [GetAthletePlural] always have a place in the spotlight.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:881`

### 中文

售卖天空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

For Sale: The Sky

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:894`

## cyber.1010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[sky_megacorp.GetName]§!向我们提供了一个利润丰厚的机遇。他们想为一种叫做“普乐快车”的全新提神饮料打广告。\n\n可以将这种饮料的广告直接投射到我们的公民的视觉植入体中，只要一抬头就能看见。所产生的收入可以用于无数的公众项目。\n\n尽管这种广告具有侵入性，但我们确信这种饮料本身应该是美味无害的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

The §Y[sky_megacorp.GetName]§! has offered us a lucrative opportunity. They wish to advertise their latest product, an energizing drink called Plasma Quench.\n\nAds for the drink will be projected straight into our citizens' ocular implants whenever they happen to glance up at the sky. The revenue generated could be used for any number of civic projects.\n\nDespite the invasive nature of the ads, we have been assured that the beverage itself is delicious, and completely non-toxic.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:895`

## cyber.1010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1010.a&lang=en)

都来喝普乐快车！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

Drink Plasma Quench!

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:896`

## cyber.1015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[sky_megacorp.GetName]§!又带来了一项新的关于全星系最受欢迎的能量饮料“普乐快车”的提案。鉴于目前对我们改造人口天空视角销售的成功，他们请求在现实世界的其他地方设置更多的广告。\n\n“[most_populous.GetName]就是最佳的广告投放地点！”一位[sky_megacorp.GetName]发言人如是说。毕竟，每个人都应该有机会获得这样令人难以置信的机会，这不是更公平吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

The §Y[sky_megacorp.GetName]§! has returned with a new offer regarding the galaxy's favorite energizing drink, Plasma Quench. Since the sale of our modified people's celestial view worked out so well, they have requested to put up additional advertisements in the physical world as well.\n\n"[most_populous.GetName] is the ideal advertisement location!" declared a [sky_megacorp.GetName] spokesperson. After all, is it not fairer that everyone should have access to such incredible opportunities?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:901`

### English

Illicit Memories

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:915`

## cyber.1030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有了如此众多能够控制感官和情感体验的植入体，强化改造的个体现在更倾向投身虚拟活动，以此进行娱乐或者逃避现实。虽然忆痕艺术家理论上可以激发出任何情绪，但这种做法却已经出现了一些黑暗的转折。\n\n忆痕俱乐部高价收购那些具有强烈情感体验的自录记忆，例如婚礼、交配仪式、自杀、袭击和谋杀等等。这一做法导致了犯罪率上升，犯罪分子不但会为了记忆犯罪，还会在这些创伤经历后窃取受害者的植入体。\n\n我们应该如何应对与之相关的乱象?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:820`

### English

With so many implants able to control sensory and emotional experiences, augmented individuals have turned to virtual life events as escapism and entertainment. While engram artists can theoretically stimulate any emotion, the practice has taken a dark turn.\n\nMemory engram clubs are paying top credits for self-recorded, intensely emotional experiences such as weddings and mating rituals, but also suicides, assaults, and murders. This has caused an uptick in crime, as criminals are both recording themselves committing offenses as well as stealing victims' implants after a traumatic experience.\n\nHow should we handle this concerning craze?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:916`

## cyber.1055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

间谍游戏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

Spy Games

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:938`

## cyber.1055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位海军军校生泄露了机密信息，其愚蠢之举令人震惊。作为一名网络战斗模拟器的忠实粉丝，这位学员与其他玩家就我们驱逐舰上炮塔的旋转速度发生了争论。\n\n为了支持自己的说法，学员甚至上传了几张我们舰船的机密蓝图，这些文件很快传遍了全星系网络。尽管肇事者已被处理，但由于这一事件，内阁中有声音呼吁全面禁止网络游戏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

In an appalling display of foolishness, a naval cadet has leaked classified information. A dedicated fan of networked combat simulators, the cadet got into an argument with other players about the angular velocity of the turrets on our destroyers.\n\nTo bolster their position, the cadet uploaded several classified blueprints of our ships, documents which quickly spread across the galactic network. Although the culprit has been dealt with, voices on the Council are calling for a wholesale ban on online gaming as a result of this incident.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:939`

### English

### 中文

事情发生就是发生了。我们会加把劲让舰队更加现代化，这样一来泄露的蓝图就没什么用了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

## cyber_breach_forgive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_breach_forgive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为响应信息泄露，我们改进了安保条款。也就是说，游戏玩家并不会对国家安全造成威胁。对这种愚蠢的过失我们没必要反应过度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

In response to the leak, we have updated our security protocols. This being said, gamers do not pose a threat to national security. There is no need to overreact to a foolish mistake.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:947`

## cyber.1205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

感官审查

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

Sensory Censorship

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:954`

## cyber.1205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最新一代的感官义体件可以让用户屏蔽不必要的感觉。包括不受欢迎的气味、不得体的画面等等，一切都可以被淡化、掩盖，甚至完全从用户的感知中抹去。我们社会中较为保守的团体越来越担心这种审查制度会对我国公民的良知产生影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

The latest generation of sensory cyberware allows user to block out unwanted sensations. From unwelcome smells to unseemly pictures, everything can be toned down, masked, or even entirely erased from the user's perception. There are growing concerns amongst the more conservative groups of our society about the effects such censorship will have on the conscience of our citizens.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:955`

## cyber.1205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须面对生命的真实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

We must face the truth of our lives.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:956`

## cyber.1205.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1205.b&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

Everyone has a right to blissful ignorance.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:957`

## cyber.1205.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1205.c&lang=en)

### 中文

美化性重渲染不错，但审查的风险就太大了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

Cosmetic reshades are fine, censoring is risky.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:958`

## cyber.1205.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1205.d&lang=en)

我们会安排好他们的感官的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:959`

## cyber_censure_truth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_censure_truth&lang=en)

### 中文

感官审查：禁用

### English

## cyber_censure_truth_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_censure_truth_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

禁止使用可以阻挡现实生活元素的认知过滤器。无论何时都应该直面现实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

People are not allowed to apply perception filters that would block out real life elements. The truth must be faced at all times.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:961`

## cyber_censure_total

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_censure_total&lang=en)

### 中文

感官审查：全面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:864`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:962`

## cyber_censure_total_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_censure_total_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果现实令人感到不适，为什么不干脆把那些不良元素屏蔽掉呢？没有必要去目睹犯罪、悲惨和偏见的画面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

### English

If reality makes you uncomfortable, why not simply block out the offending elements? You need not witness crime, misery or prejudice.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:963`

## cyber_censure_partial

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_censure_partial&lang=en)

### 中文

感官重渲染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:866`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:964`

## cyber_censure_partial_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_censure_partial_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们允许人们使用植入体的认知重渲染功能。他们可以在单调的生活中注入灵动的色彩。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:867`

### English

We have allowed people to use perception reshades in their implants. With this, they can inject lively colors and effects in their otherwise drab existence.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:965`

## cyber_censure_authoritarian_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber_censure_authoritarian_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正在安排员工看待现实的方式。通过将政治异见信息和人物挡在我们忠诚公民的生活之外，就可以保护他们不受那些可能产生误导的人的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

We are curating how our people perceive reality. By blocking out dissident messages and actors from the lives of our loyal citizens, we preserve them from those that would misguide them.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:967`

## cyber.1210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，雄心勃勃的企业和地下运动的广告倾向于在用户的义体件中隐秘地植入信息。虚假记忆、选择性荷尔蒙反应和睡眠建议正变得司空见惯。忧心忡忡的政界人士向我们提出了这个关切，并主张禁止这些侵入性信息，或至少加以监管。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

A recent trend in advertisement for underground movements and ambitious corporations is to implant surreptitious messages in the user cyberware. Fake memories, selective hormonal reactions, and sleep suggestions are becoming commonplace. Concerned political actors have raised the issue with us and are advocating for a ban, or at least a regulation these invasive messages. 

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:970`

义体安全漏洞

### English

Cyber Security Breach

## cyber.1255.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1255.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，有几份机密文件被远程服务器非法下载。我们的安全人员坚持认为所有的主防火墙和二级防火墙都完好无损，到目前为止也没有外部网络攻击的证据。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

Recently, several classified documents were illegally downloaded by a remote server. Our security operatives are adamant that all primary and secondary firewalls remain intact, and so far there is no evidence of an external cyber attack.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:985`

## cyber.1255.gov.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1255.gov.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.1255.intro$但结论无可逃避：我们中出了个叛徒，而且鉴于这些信息的敏感程度，他一定有着很高的权限。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

$cyber.1255.intro$The conclusion is inescapable: we have a leak, and given the sensitive nature of the stolen information, it must be someone with high-level clearance.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:986`

## cyber.1255.hack.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1255.hack.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.1255.intro$但结论无可逃避：我们有漏洞——这个漏洞让我们接受开源义体强化件的决定受到质疑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

$cyber.1255.intro$The conclusion is inescapable: we have a leak - one that casts our decision to embrace open-sourced cybernetic augmentation into doubt.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:987`

### English

Launch an investigation.

## leak_investigation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleak_investigation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了找出漏洞，我们需要调查所有安装了义体植入体的人。这个过程很可能会比较……侵入性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

To uncover the leak, we will need to investigate all those with cybernetic implants. The process is likely to be... intrusive.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:990`

## cyber.1260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神经巫术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

Neuromancy

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:992`

## cyber.1260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对义体安全漏洞的调查获得了结论。\n\n我们已经锁定了一种强化件和一些政府高级官员，他们的神经学湿件遭到了入侵。外国特工对其进行操作，并强迫他们违背自己的意志行动，这些个体对上述事件没有任何记忆。\n\n尽管我们已经知道了泄露的原因，但我们对究竟是谁——或什么东西——进行了窃密仍一筹莫展。然而，通过秘密向那些受影响的官员意识中植入错误信息，我们也许可以追溯到窃密源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

The investigation into our cyber security leak has concluded.\n\nCertain augmented and high-level officials within the government have been targeted, their neurological wetware hacked. Manipulated by external agents and forced to act against their will, these individuals have no memory of the events in question.\n\nAlthough we now know the cause of the leak, we are no closer to discovering who - or what - was ultimately responsible. However, by surreptitiously implanting false data into the minds of the affected officials, we may be able to trace the leak back to its source.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:993`

## cyber.1265.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1265.name&lang=en)

### 中文

精神劫持者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

Mind Jackers

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1000`

## cyber.1265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

找到了。\n\n藏身于本次史无前例网络攻击背后的犯罪分子已经被找到了。这些自称为“精神劫持者”的人除了制造混乱没有其他目的，他们能游刃有余地绕过我们的安全系统。虽然主犯已经被拘留，但我们仍然必须决定如何处理我们受侵害的官员。\n\n最简单的解决方案是在受影响的植入体周围安装自定义防火墙。而还有一个更干净、但风险更大的选择是硬重启：通过进入上述领袖的基础认知功能，重新安装操作系统，期间这些领袖会被强制“关机”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

We have them.\n\nThe criminals behind the unprecedented cyber attacks have been found. With no goal other than the sowing of chaos, these self-proclaimed 'Mind Jackers' managed to circumvent our security systems with embarrassing ease. While the ringleaders are already in custody, we must still decide what to do with our compromised officials.\n\nThe simplest solution is to install a custom firewall around their affected implants. A cleaner, though more risky, option is a hard reboot: accessing their base cognitive functions, the leaders in question could be forcibly 'shut down' while a new operating system is installed.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1001`

## cyber.1265.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1265.a&lang=en)

### 中文

建立防火墙。

### English

### English

### English

## cyber.1270.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1270.name&lang=en)

### 中文

重装系统；重获新生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:903`

### English

Rebooted; Renewed

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1006`

## cyber.1270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对入侵的官员的硬重启导致了一些意想不到的后果：虽然抹去了所有神经系统入侵的痕迹，但他们积累的一大部分知识和经验也随之而去。\n\n尽管遭遇挫折，但在“重新开始”的过程中，这些领袖表示自己在职业和个人方面都获得了深刻的新生。通过迫使我们采取如此激烈的措施，黑客们可能无意中帮了我们一个忙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

The hard reboot of our compromised officials has resulted in some unexpected consequences: every trace of the neurological hack has been wiped clean, but so too has a great portion of their accumulated knowledge and experience.\n\nDespite this setback, in 'starting fresh' these leaders report deep feelings of professional and personal renewal. By forcing us to take such drastic measures, the hackers may have unintentionally done us a favor.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1007`

## rebooted_leaders

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arebooted_leaders&lang=en)

### 中文

移除具有§Y$leader_trait_hacked$§!特质领袖的所有§H负面特质§!。\n每移除一项特质造成§R300§!经验值的损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

Removes all §HNegative Traits§! from Leaders with the §Y$leader_trait_hacked$§! Trait.\nFor each Trait removed, §R300§! Experience will be lost.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1008`

## cyber.1275.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1275.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神经防火墙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

Neurological Firewalls

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1011`

## cyber.1275.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1275.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的意识安全了。\n\n湿件工程师成功地设计出了神经学防火墙。为了维持我们的集体安全，我们目前和未来的所有官员立刻安装上了这些程序。尽管这会对我们的领导阶层的基础认知功能造成影响，但这项工程也对数据安全科学带来了卓著的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

Our minds are safe.\n\nWetware engineers have successfully designed a neurological firewall. In order to maintain our collective security, the program will be immediately installed in all current and future officials. While this will have a detrimental impact on the base cognitive functionality of our leadership class, the project has provided significant insight into the science of data security.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1012`

### English

## leader_trait_firewalled_desc

## cyber.1505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

社会2.0

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

Society 2.0

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1020`

## cyber.1505.desc.individualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1505.desc.individualist&lang=en)

### 中文

民主过程是这个社会的基础，显然只有加强这个基础才是明智的选择。新的义体使我们能够对或大或小的所有社会问题进行投票，为个人提供前所未有的表达意见的机会。这带来了难以想象的数据财富和无穷的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:916`

### English

The democratic process is what this society has been built on. It only makes sense to strengthen this foundation. Our new cybernetics enable us to cast our vote on all societal matters big or small, affording the individual unprecedented opportunities to express their opinion. The wealth of data this brings is unimaginable and the possibilities are boundless.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1021`

## cyber.1505.desc.collectivist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1505.desc.collectivist&lang=en)

### 中文

公开地向其他的§Y[Species.GetNamePlural]§!同胞传播自己的想法看似是一种糟糕前景。但最初的震惊很快就会被对周围环境的自信和确定感所取代。我们知道身边人在做什么，反之他们也一样。不需要言语，只需一动念头，交流就能瞬间完成。为了保护这种清晰的思维，义体大脑的情绪功能被指令关闭以免淹没重要信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

Openly broadcasting your thoughts to your fellow §Y[Species.GetNamePlural]§! is a harrowing prospect. But the initial shock is soon replaced with a sense of confidence and certainty over one's surroundings. We are aware of what the person next to us does, and they are aware of us. Communication happens instantly without words with a mere thought. To protect this clarity of mind, the cyberbrain has been instructed to nullify emotions lest they drown what is important.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1022`

## cyber.1505.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1505.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管经过了无数次的磋商、投票和热烈的讨论，国家仍未就义体强化社会的问题做出决定。为了避免使这个过程演变成一场分裂的争论，领导层必须介入。\n\n我们可以利用这个机会修改义体，为民主过程服务，或者采取更激进的方法，让人们通过他们的义体件分享想法。后者还需要抑制义体大脑基本情绪，以防止个人强烈的自发想法扰乱其周围的环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:918`

### English

Despite endless consultation, rounds of voting and lively discourse, the nation remains undecided on the matters of cyber-enhanced society. Before the process devolves into a divisive argument, the leadership must step in.\n\nWe can use this opportunity to modify our cybernetics to serve the democratic process or take the more radical approach where people share their thoughts via their cyberware. The latter would also require the cyberbrain to suppress base emotions to prevent forceful spontaneous thoughts from disrupting one's immediate surroundings.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1023`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

权力结构重构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:926`

### English

Power Restructured

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1032`

## cyber.1510.desc.individualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1510.desc.individualist&lang=en)

### 中文

很显然，大众都想要出人头地、与其他人一争高下。我们迎合这种超竞争行为的方式是设立一种游戏化的系统，用社会流动性和小饰品的承诺来换取生产力的提高，也能很好地满足他们的愿望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

It has become clear that the population wishes to stand out and joust against one another. Our plans to support this hyper-competitive behavior with a gamified system, which promises social mobility and minor trinkets in exchange for increased productivity, will suit their desire well.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1033`

## cyber.1510.desc.collectivist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1510.desc.collectivist&lang=en)

### 中文

人们所能感受到的集体意识比个人的社会流动性更重要。因此，我们将挖掘睡眠增强大脑的潜力，而不是利用竞争本能。将计算能力通过义体上行链路无线连接到基站，在对象的整个休息阶段中下达计算任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

That the people feel a sense of community is more important than individual social mobility. So, instead of leveraging competitiveness, we will tap into the potential of the sleeping augmented brain. Its computational power is connected wirelessly to a base station via a cybernetic uplink, which feeds computational tasks to the subject throughout the rest phase.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1034`

## cyber.1510.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1510.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的国民拒绝对社会应变得更具竞争性或是协作性的问题表明立场。而作为他们的上级，我们现在将为他们决定最好的方案。\n\n我们可以创建一个高度竞争性的游戏化系统，在这个系统中，比你的同行表现得更好可能会为职业生涯带来上升机会，或者通过义体上行链路利用整个社区的计算能力，将他们的大脑在沉睡中连接到我们的服务器农场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:929`

### English

Our subjects have failed to make a stand either towards a more competitive or communal society. As their superiors, We will now decide the best course of action for them.\n\nWe can either create a highly competitive gamified system where performing better than your piers might yield an upward trajectory for their careers or instead harness the computational power of the entire community via a cybernetic uplink to connect their sleeping brains into our server farms.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1035`

## cyber.1515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

人群控制

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:937`

### English

Crowd Controlled

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1044`

## cyber.1515.desc.individualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1515.desc.individualist&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Species.GetAdj]§!民众正在逐渐接受一个可以用强化体以全新的方式表达自我的世界。\n\n但这种宽容只是表象，植入体的内增强监控算法现在正在监视着每一个人，在大脑突触连接的那一刻就能发现不当的想法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:938`

### English

The §Y[Species.GetAdj]§! people are embracing a world where the augmented can express their identity in brand new ways.\n\nBut the leniency is only a facade, as enhanced surveillance algorithms within the implant are now monitoring every individual, detecting dissident thoughts the moment brain synapses connect.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1045`

## cyber.1515.desc.collectivist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1515.desc.collectivist&lang=en)

### 中文

危难当前，唯有团结。每一个通过义体增强的§Y[Species.GetAdj]§!都在为更美好的未来而奋斗，并相信身边的人也都会这样做。\n\n具有钢铁般意志和光辉人格的§Y[Ruler.GetName][Ruler.GetTitle]§!会带领我们的人民向前进，他们大可对此放心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:939`

### English

There is strength in numbers and its unifying momentum in the face of adversity. Every cyber-enhanced §Y[Species.GetAdj]§! fights towards a better future together, trusting the person next to them to do the same.\n\nOur people can rest secure in the knowledge that the radiant personality of their iron-willed §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetName]§! guides them onwards.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1046`

## cyber.1515.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1515.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

当人民继续在十字路口犹豫不决时时，我们的§Y[Ruler.GetName][Ruler.GetTitle]§!必须抓住强化改造所提供的这一独特机会并作出决定。\n\n应该让我们的人民保持一定的自由意志，以植入体进行监控，或是要让他们完全由领袖光芒四射的意志所引领。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

As the people continue to waver in the crossroads, our §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetName]§! must seize this unique opportunity offered by the augmentations and make a decision.\n\nShould our people be allowed some personal freedoms while monitoring them through their implants, or have them influenced by the will of their leader's radiant personality?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1047`

## cyber.1520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国万岁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:951`

### English

Long Live the Empire

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1059`

## cyber.1520.desc.individualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1520.desc.individualist&lang=en)

### 中文

皇帝下诏要求我们把精力集中在王朝本身。一块特殊的§Y$r_the_rulerchip$§!会被植入每一位未来的统治者，以记录并转移他们作为§Y[root.GetName]§!的[root.GetRulerTitle]期间的经验。\n\n随着$r_the_rulerchip$的代代相传，其蕴含的丰富经验将成为我们社会的重要记录。另外，我们也可以利用人民的知识来增益领袖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:952`

### English

The imperial seat has decreed our efforts be focused on the dynasty itself. A special §Y$r_the_rulerchip$§! will be implanted on all future rulers to record and transfer their experiences as the [root.GetRulerTitle] of the §Y[root.GetName]§!. This relic chip will then be transferred to the heir at the time of the ruler's passing.\n\nAs the $r_the_rulerchip$ is passed on through generations, its vast library of experiences will become the foundational record of our society. We can also leverage our population's learnings for our leaders' benefit.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1060`

## cyber.1520.desc.collectivist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1520.desc.collectivist&lang=en)

### 中文

为了缓解我们帝国公民的辛劳，必须在§Y[root.Capital.GetName]§!/“建立一座宏伟的§Y$building_cyberdome$§!。它将会成为新式义体运动的举办场所，强化过的运动员们将为了我们的青睐和景仰争奇斗艳。目睹如此尖端的竞争无疑能激起大众对§Y[root.GetName]§!的崇拜之情。\n\n此外，这些景象还可以通过神经广播直接传达到全帝国的义体大脑中，驱使热情的人群前往[root.Capital.GetName]，亲身体验这种行为的原始刺激。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:953`

### English

To offer our imperial citizens respite from their toil, a great §Y$building_cyberdome$§! must be built on §Y[root.Capital.GetName]§!. It will serve as the glorious venue for new kinds of cybersports, where augmented athletes compete for our favor and admiration. Witnessing such fierce competition will surely invigorate the masses and their admiration for the §Y[root.GetName]§!.\n\nFurthermore, these spectacles can be neurocasted straight into the cyberbrain, which will compel the exuberant crowds to travel to [root.Capital.GetName] and experience the primal thrill of the action firsthand.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1061`

## cyber.1520.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1520.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

我们社会在义体领域的发展走到了一个岔路口。我们可以致力于造福少数人或是大众上。无论作何选择都将为§Y[root.GetName]§!带来好处。\n\n开发一种专业的植入体可以让我们将[root.GetRulerTitle]的经验传承给子嗣。而着力发展强化义体运动和广播科技则可以让我们可以在[root.Capital.GetName]的首都举办盛大的赛事活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

Our societal advancement in the field of cybernetics is at a crossroads. We can either concentrate our efforts for the benefit of the few or the many. Both would ultimately serve the §Y[root.GetName]§! well.\n\nDeveloping a specialized implant would allow us to transfer the experiences of the [root.GetRulerTitle] to the heir. Emphasis on augmented cybersports and broadcasting technologies would allow us to organize magnificent events on our capital of [root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1062`

## cyber.1525.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1525.name&lang=en)

### 中文

植入体入股

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:962`

### English

Implants Incorporated

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1071`

## cyber.1525.desc.individualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1525.desc.individualist&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Species.GetAdj]§!焦点小组对他们的新眼部植入物反应积极。我们的分析部门认为，广告效果显著提高，整个产品组合的销售额有望大幅增长。这为即将到来的完整产品发布展示了巨大的潜力。\n\n义体时代已经来临，我们已经做好充分准备以一种全新的企业战略来加以利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

§Y[Species.GetAdj]§! focus groups are responding positively to the their new ocular implants. Our analytics department suggests a remarkable surge in ad effectiveness and a promising bump in sales throughout our entire portfolio. This showcases immense potential for the forthcoming full product launch.\n\nThe age of cybernetics is here, and we are fully poised to capitalize it with a renewed corporate strategy.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1072`

## cyber.1525.desc.collectivist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1525.desc.collectivist&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对整体自我完善的承诺引起了大众的共鸣。\n\n关注社会经济问题密切地贴合我们企业的愿景，这可以确保必要植入体不断地推陈出新。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:964`

### English

Our commitment to holistic self-improvement is resonating with the general population.\n\nAttending to socio-economic concerns aligns with our corporate vision, ensuring that new generations of must-have implants are introduced on a constant basis.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1073`

## cyber.1525.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.1525.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

考虑到义体技术给社会带来的巨大变化，普通消费者会纠结于方向的选择或许也就不足为奇了。我们应该将个体差异作为标准，还是更应该关注集体福祉?\n\n这个关键而无与伦比的决定必然会产生深远的影响，但优柔寡断就会损失利益；必须尽快做出抉择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:965`

### English

Given the vast changes that cybernetic technology have introduced to society, perhaps it should come as no surprise that the average consumer is torn about which direction to take. Will individual differentiation become our standard, or should focus be placed on the collective well-being?\n\nThis pivotal and unparalleled decision is sure to have far-reaching consequences, but indecision is the death of profit; a choice must be made.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1074`

## situation_cyberization_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_cyberization_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢思维需要优越的躯体。有了义体植入体，我们就可以对工蜂进行改进、强化、重塑。\n是时候超越我们生物学的极限了。\n\n§Y次月获得植入体的人口数§!：§G[From.GetCyberPopsPerMonth]§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:976`

### English

A Hive Mind needs superior bodies. With cybernetic implants, our drones can be perfected, enhanced, remodelled.\nIt is time to transcend the limits of our biology.\n\n§YPops to receive implants next month§!: §G[From.GetCyberPopsPerMonth]§!

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1087`

### 中文

躯体万千，意志独一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1025`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1142`

## cyber.2100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当在思考增强改造的本质时，我们也发现自己其实正在探索自己的愿望。\n\n义体是达到目的的一种手段：它是一种优化我们的身体以更好地执行意志的方法。无限的强化改造是可能的，每一个强化体都将带来新的优势，但也带来了新的要求。机械肢体很重，网络大脑需要更多的能量，精密的部件也需要不断的维护。在通往完美的道路上，必须做出抉择。\n\n要超越[Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed]局限、让机械化的[owner.GetSpeciesHandName]触及寰宇，我们的雄心壮志到底要聚焦何处？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1026`

### English

As we ponder the nature of augmentation, we find ourselves exploring our aspirations.\n\nCyberization is a mean to an end: a way to optimize our bodies to better execute our will. Endless forms of augmentation are possible, each bringing new strengths, but also needs. Mechanical limbs are heavy, cyberbrains require more energy, and delicate components require constant maintenance. On the road to perfection, choices must be made.\n\nWhat is the focus of our ambition, the one goal that requires transcending the limits of the [Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed] to stretch a metallic [owner.GetSpeciesHandName] across the sky?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1143`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着闲散的思考变成具体的计划，一种迫切的需求出现了：一个专门用于实验以及测试、建造义体原型的空间。\n\n我们将建立一座$building_augmentation_center$，这样我们就可以把我们的[Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed]拆开，用金属和梦想重新编织在一起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1035`

### English

As idle musings turn into concrete plans, a pressing need arises: a space devoted to experimentation, where cybernetic prototypes can be tested and built.\n\nWe shall raise an $building_augmentation_center$, so we may rend our [Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed] apart and reknit it together with metal and dreams.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1153`

是时候开始建设未来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1036`

### English

## cyber.2205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

原型万千，唯选其一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1038`

### English

Many Prototypes. One Choice.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1157`

## cyber.2205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的义体研究已经产出了多个有前途的义体原型供我们选择。初步结果表明，一些原型机在体力方面表现出色，而另一些则更适合精密工作。每一种都有其长处和局限性，没有一种具有压倒性优势。\n\n我们意识中浮现出一个令人生畏的提议：我们可以让子个体变得多样化、根据功能不同的来改变身体吗？我们或许将变得不再完全相同。这样的未来难以把控，但也许对于我们整体的效率提高而言这是值得的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1039`

### English

Our cybernetic research has produced multiple promising cyborg prototypes for our consideration. Preliminary results show that some prototypes excel at physical feats, while others are more suited for precision work. Each has its strengths and limitations, none showing clear superiority.\n\nA daunting proposal springs to our mind: Could we, perhaps, diversify our drones? Modify our bodies in different ways depending on their function? We won't be all identical anymore, a prospect we find hard to grasp. But the efficiency gain might make it worth our harmony.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1158`

### English

### English

## cyber.2220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

这也能是我们的一员吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1042`

### English

One Of Us?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1162`

## cyber.2220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在组装工蜂的过程中偶尔会有存在缺陷的躯体。通常这些畸形的工蜂都会被处理掉，但多亏了义体植入体，它们仍可以加入到我们的行列中。提高我们的数量是好事，但有缺陷的工蜂需要根据问题的严重程度进行个性化植入。我们的蜂巢会成长得更快，但也会变得更……不完美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1043`

### English

Drone assembly occasionally results in defective bodies. Misshapen drones are usually disposed of, but thanks to cybernetic implants, they could be allowed to join our ranks. Improving our numbers is desirable, but faulty drones require personalized implants depending on the severity of their issues. Our Hive would grow faster, but also become more... imperfect.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1163`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2220.a&lang=en)

人多力量大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1044`

### English

There is strength in numbers.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1164`

### English

An imperfect Hive is a weak Hive.

## cyber.2260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隐性问题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1046`

### English

One Invisible Issue

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1167`

## cyber.2260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一小部分受试对象遭受了对强化改造的不良反应。他们的身体排斥植入体，发生了危及生命的并发症。其病因似乎是一种以前未被注意到的遗传缺陷。根据初步计算，这大约会影响5%的工蜂人口。\n\n在确定了问题之后，我们现在可以对工蜂进行筛查加以预防，但不知道该如何处理受影响的人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1047`

### English

A small percentage of our test subjects are suffering adverse reactions to augmentation. Their bodies are rejecting the implants, bringing life-threatening side-effects. The cause seems to be a genetic defect, previously unnoticed. According to preliminary calculations, this affects around 5% of our drone population.\n\nHaving identified the issue, we can now screen our drones to prevent it, but we don't know what to do with the affected population.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1168`

## cyber.2300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工蜂万千，计划独一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1056`

### English

Many Drones. One Plan.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1178`

## cyber.2300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的义体原型通过了批准，§Y$building_augmentation_center$§!也已经建好。我们的计划万事俱备，只剩下我们转化的后勤工作还没最终敲定下来。\n\n我们可以下令所有的工蜂前往$building_augmentation_center$以进行转化流程，暂停他们的日常活动。这个过程会非常快速，但期间会让所有的工厂都停止运转。或者我们还可以安排工蜂的日日构成，在他们的强制休息期间进行义体人转化，以避免影响日常活动。这种计划尽管慢了点儿，但影响也小一些。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1057`

### English

Our cyborg prototypes are approved. The §Y$building_augmentation_center$§! has been built. Our plan is ready, but the logistics of our transformation are yet to be finalized.\n\nWe could order all our drones to head to an $building_augmentation_center$ and undergo the procedure, suspending their daily activities. This process would be very fast, but would leave our factories empty until it is complete. Alternatively, we could work around our drones' schedules, turning them into cyborgs during their mandatory resting periods in order to not impact their activities. Although slower, this plan is less disruptive.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1179`

## cyber.2320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

众惧者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1063`

### English

The Many Fearing One

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1186`

## cyber.2320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由单一实体组成的帝国以怀疑的目光注视着我们，恐惧于我们的人口众多。而义体植入体可以让我们的特使具有更独特的面孔和个性，呈现出不那么同质的外表。\n\n这些分裂体的国家终将会感激我们的努力，甚至可能开始……同情我们，把我们看作他们的同类。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1064`

### English

Empires composed of singular entities watch us with suspicion, fearing our plurality. With cybernetic implants, we could give our envoys more distinct faces and personalities, presenting a less homogeneous facade.\n\nThe disunited would appreciate the effort. They might even start to... empathize with us. See us as one of them.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1187`

### English

## cyber.2360.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2360.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一种人口，两个群体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

One Population, Two Groups

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1194`

## cyber.2360.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2360.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们蜂巢的义体化正在顺利进行中。我们的一部分人口现在已经搭载了义体植入体，而另一部分则还是完全的生物体。我们从未体在自己的身体上验过这样强烈的差异。工蜂现在可以将自己识别为不同群体的一部分，这种分裂感正在诱发它们的个体意识。\n\n有些工蜂开始向自己身上安装小配件以装饰自己，或在植入体上绘制特殊符号以区分彼此。真是一种有趣但又令人担忧的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English

The cyberization of our Hive is continuing smoothly. Part of our population now carries cybernetic implants, while others are still completely biological. Never before have we experienced such striking differences between our selves. Drones are now able to identify themselves as part of one or the other group, and this split is expanding their sense of individuality.\n\nSome drones have started to adorn themselves with small accessories, or are painting symbols on their implants to tell each other apart. It's an intriguing, yet unnerving development.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1195`

## cyber.2360.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2360.a&lang=en)

### 中文

鼓励这种发展。

### English

Encourage this development.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1196`

## cyber.2400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

思绪万千，大脑独一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1076`

### English

Many Thoughts. One Brain.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1201`

## cyber.2400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

安装神经植入体对工蜂造成了意料外的影响：由于大脑变成了半义体，他们现在可以独立于我们的主意识之外产生个体意识。\n\n我们可以进一步开发这种科技，提升工蜂的自主思考能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1077`

### English

Installing neural implants into our drones led to an unforeseen result: since their brains are now partly cybernetic, they can now form individual thoughts separated from our main consciousness.\n\nWe could further develop this technology, improving our drones' capability for autonomous thinking.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1202`

## cyber.2400.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2400.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们的工蜂自己思考。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1078`

### English

Let our drones think for themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1203`

## cyber.2400.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2400.b&lang=en)

### 中文

个体主义对我们的团结是一个威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1079`

### English

Individuality is a threat to our unity.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1204`

## cyber.2420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2420.name&lang=en)

### 中文

所有人的终点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1080`

### English

One End For Everyone

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1206`

## cyber.2420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

义体植入体让死亡这件事变得复杂了起来。\n\n将植入体从死亡的工蜂身上拆下可以让我们进行循环利用，减少浪费。我们的整个废弃物抛弃流程需要进行优化以兼容这一新的步骤；需要花费的资源较多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1081`

### English

Cybernetic implants are complicating the logistic of death.\n\nStripping deceased drones of their implants would allow us to reuse the technology, reducing waste. Our whole waste disposal process would have to get an overhaul to accommodate this new step; the resource cost is significant.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1207`

## cyber.2420.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2420.a&lang=en)

### 中文

回收植入体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1082`

### English

Recycle the implants.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1208`

## cyber.2420.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2420.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不值得如此大费周章。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1083`

### English

It's not worth the cost.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1209`

## cyber.2420.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2420.c&lang=en)

### 中文

回收植入体……还有躯壳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1084`

### English

Recycle the implants... and the bodies.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1210`

## cyber.2460.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2460.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一生的植入体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1085`

### English

Implants For One Lifetime

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1212`

## cyber.2460.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2460.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现有的人口正在稳步地进行义体化，但必须为还未出生的人口建立一种流程。\n\n以我们的科技，我们甚至可以在[owner.Species.GetSpawnNamePlural]出生前就安装上义体植入体，从而创造一个真正义体化的物种：这种方案快速、轻松且高效。另一方面，初步研究表明，在极早期安装植入体可能造成精神分裂、精神错乱、震颤和各种发育障碍。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1086`

### English

Our existing population is undergoing cyberization at a steady rate, but we must establish a process for those yet to be born.\n\nWith our technology, we can fit our [owner.Species.GetSpawnNamePlural] with cybernetic implants before they are even born, creating a truly cybernetic species: a quick, easy and efficient solution. On the other hand, preliminary research show that installing implant at an early age early could cause dissociation, psychosis, tremors, and assorted developmental disorders.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1213`

## cyber.2460.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2460.a&lang=en)

### 中文

金属强过[Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1087`

### English

Metal is stronger than [Owner.Species.GetFleshTypeCyberCreed].

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1214`

## cyber.2460.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2460.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为义体化设立最低年龄限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1088`

### English

Establish a minimum age before cyberization.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1215`

## cyber.2004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工蜂万千，意志独一？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1090`

### English

Many Drones. One Will?

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1218`

## cyber.2004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

转化已经完成了。义体植入体经过了充分的测试、原型设计、优化、打磨并进行了安装，将我们的身体变为了完美的版本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1091`

### English

The transformation is complete. Cybernetic implants have been tested, prototyped, refined, polished and installed, turning our bodies into perfected versions of themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1219`

## cyber.2004.desc.volitional

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2004.desc.volitional&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.2004.desc$\n\n我们的工蜂现在具有更强的个体思维能力——更多的自由也带来了新的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1092`

### English

$cyber.2004.desc$\n\nOur drones are now more capable of individual thoughts - and with greater freedom comes new possibilities.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1220`

## cyber.2004.desc.integrated

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2004.desc.integrated&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.2004.desc$\n\n我们的工蜂比以往更加地团结了，金属的纽带强化了意识的联结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1093`

### English

$cyber.2004.desc$\n\nOur drones are now more united than ever, metallic chains strengthening the bond of the mind.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1221`

## cyber.2004.desc.neutral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2004.desc.neutral&lang=en)

### 中文

$cyber.2004.desc$\n\n在这个过中，我们发现了改变的机遇。我们的团结中蕴含着力量，但更多的自主工蜂也带来了一定的优势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1094`

### English

$cyber.2004.desc$\n\nDuring this process, we saw opportunities for change. There is strength in our union, but more autonomous drones also have their advantages.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1222`

## cyber.2004.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2004.end&lang=en)

随着我们身体的演化，我们的蜂巢意识也可以发生蜕变。

As our bodies evolve, so could our Hive Mind become something more.

## cyber.2010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

彰显意图

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1103`

### English

Manifest Purpose

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1232`

## cyber.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着先进义体的出现，我们意识到自己的终极潜力。而到目前为止，我们还只触及了表面。\n\n随着专业知识的积累和技术的进步，我们的社会必将发生变化。对智慧生命的完全整合我们势在必行。\n\n抵抗毫无意义。全星系的心智都会是我们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1104`

### English

With the advent of advanced cybernetics, we can realize our ultimate potential. Up to now, we have only scratched the surface.\n\nAs our expertise increases and technology improves, our society is sure to be transformed. The total assimilation of intelligent life is our destiny.\n\nResistance is meaningless. The minds of the galaxy are ours to employ.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1233`

## cyber.2015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

先进同化者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1105`

### English

Advanced Assimilators

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1234`

## cyber.2015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

与机器融合后，我们的有机人口达到了近乎完美的状态。现在是时候将义体也整合进我们文明的织构之中了。\n\n将同化人口的思维敏锐度进行简化，可以创造“聚合性记忆”，将他们的集体经验用来提升后勤能力。或者，我们可以通过促进“神经合唱”的创造，允许思想和情绪反应有更大的差异。\n\n这两种情况都肯定会对我们的未来产生深远的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1106`

### English

Fused with the machine, our organic population has achieved a state of near perfection. The time has come to incorporate advanced cybernetics into the very fabric of our civilization.\n\nBy streamlining the mental acuity of our cyborgs, we could form an 'Memory in Aggregate', channeling their collected experience into increased logistical capacity. Alternatively, we could allow for greater variance in thought and emotional response by facilitating the creation of a 'Neural Chorus.'\n\nEither course is sure to have a profound effect on our future.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1235`

## cyber.2015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acyber.2015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

整合我们的思想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1107`

### English

Aggregate our minds.

## gov_memory_aggregator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agov_memory_aggregator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将同化人口的思维敏锐度进行简化，“聚合性记忆”便形成了，这能够极大地改善所有的行政和后勤任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/machine_age_situations_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1114`

### English

By streamlining the mental acuity of an assimilated population, a 'Memory in Aggregate' is formed, one capable of greatly improving all administrative and logistical tasks.

Source: `localisation/english/machine_age_situations_1_l_english.yml:1243`
